जबकी सक्षम प्राधिकारी को धारा ३-ग के तहत दायर आपत्ती प्राप्त हुई है,जिस पर विचार किया गया है और 
उसका उचित रूप से निपटान किया गया;

और सक्षम प्राधिकारी ने उक्त अधिनियम की धारा ३ घ की उपधारा (2) के अनुसरण में, केन्द्रीय सरकार को 
अपनी रिपोर्ट दे दी है; 


अतः अब, केन्द्रीय सरकार, सक्षम प्राधिकारी की उक्त रिपोर्ट प्राप्त हो जाने पर और उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 
३ घ की उपधारा (१) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए यह घोषणा करती है कि उक्त अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमि 
का पूर्वोक्त प्रयोजन के लिए अर्जन किया जाना चाहिए ; 

और अब केन्द्रीय सरकार, उक्त अधिनियम की धारा ३ घ की उपधारा (२) के अनुसरण में, यह घोषणा करता 
है. कि इस अधिसूचना के राजपत्र में प्रकाशन पर उक्त अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमि, सभी विल्लंगमों से मुक्त हो कर 
आत्यन्तिक रूप से केन्द्रीय सरकार में निहित हो जाएगी |

36 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART II—SEC. 30) | 


MINISTRY OF ROAD TRANSPORT AND HIGHWAYS 
NOTIFICATION 
New Delhi, the 5th December, 2025 


S.O. 5787(E).— Whereas by the notification of the Government of India in the Ministry of Road Transport 
and Highways number S.0.433(E) dated 26/03/2025published in Gazette of India, Extraordinary, Part II, Section 3, 
Sub-section (ii) issued under sub-section (l) ofsection 3A of the National Highways Act, 956 (48 of 956) 
(hereinafter referred to as the said Act), the Central Government declared its intentionto acquire the land specified in 
the Schedule annexed to the said notification for building (widening / two lane with paved shoulder/ fourwith earthen 
shoulder etc.) maintenance, management and operation of National Highway No.-60 SPUR on the stretch of land 
fromKm. 2+928 to Km. 63+378 Bhimashankar —Taleghar-Wada- Rajgurunagar Road in the District of Pune in the 
state of Maharashtra. 


And whereas the substance of the said notification S.O.l433 (E) dated 26/03/2025 has been published in 
“Business Standard (Englishy’And “Dainik Kesari (Marathi)” both are Dated 07/04/2025 ; under sub-section (3) of 
section 3A of the said Act; 


And whereas the Competent Authority has received objections filed under Section 3-C, considered and 
settled the same appropriately; 


And whereas, in pursuance of sub-section (l) of section 3D of the said Act, the competent authority has 
submitted its report to the Central Government; 


Now, therefore, upon receipt of the said report of the competent authority and in exercise of the powers 
conferred by the sub-section (l) of section 3D of the said Act, the Central Government hereby declares that the land 
specified in the said Schedule should be acquired for the aforesaid purpose; 


And further, in pursuance of sub-section (2) of section 3D of the said Act, the Central Government hereby 
declares that on publication of this notification in the Official Gazette, the land specified in the said Schedule shall 
vest absolutely in the Central Government, free from all encumbrances.
